# Skills · G · part 120

1,025 entries, grill-workflow to grim-polish. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grill-workflow**: Clarify product, domain, scope, constraints, business rules, and technology decisions through dependency-aware question sets. Read the repository first, ask all useful questions whose prerequisites are already known in each set, and use the answers to form later sets. Use when a project or feature is ambiguous and not ready for specification. When decisions are stable, recommend exactly one next skill without invoking it. ([phatysddev/skills](https://github.com/phatysddev/skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/grill-workflow) · ★8)
- **grill-workflow**: Workflow grilling session for operators, owners, and stakeholders. Interview a business about how a workflow actually runs, sharpen fuzzy terminology, build a per-engagement glossary inline, and record load-bearing business decisions. Use when scoping a consulting engagement around a workflow, running an operations interview, stress-testing how an executive's team actually executes a process, or maintaining a per-client domain glossary. ([seanng/hq2](https://github.com/seanng/hq2/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/grill-workflow))
- **grill-writing**: Interview the author about a new section of their manuscript until the two of you share what it says, then write it - the grilling repurposed for writing (ADR-0012). Use when the author says "grill me about this section", "interview me before I write", "let's write a new section", or invokes /grill-writing, optionally with a chapter id (CHP-nnnn) and a source id (SRC-nnnnnn) the app's New section dialog printed. Not for editing prose that exists - that is a rewrite under an authorization. ([JddAndrewLauren/memoria](https://github.com/JddAndrewLauren/memoria/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill-writing))
- **grill-x**: 計画・設計をユーザーと共通認識に達するまで10の質疑で徹底的に詰めるための議論スキル。 ([mochimochiki/.claude](https://github.com/mochimochiki/.claude/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-x))
- **grill-you**: Flesh out a high-level task by spawning a sub-claude that runs `grill-with-docs` on the topic, then answering its questions on behalf of the user autonomously. Produces a task file specific enough for autonomous implementation. ([mmkal/agents](https://github.com/mmkal/agents/tree/HEAD/global/skills/grill-you))
- **grill-your-own-work**: Adversarial closer — invoke AFTER work is "complete and verified" and BEFORE saying done, /ship, or opening a PR, on any diff spanning 2+ files or platforms; also when asked to "grill", "audit", "self grill" or "adversarially review" anyone's work. Fresh gates, mandate-vs-reality, the design as well as the diff, suspicion-driven probes, REPORT-ONLY reviewers, a graded verdict. ([VoMinhKhoii/Kallo](https://github.com/VoMinhKhoii/Kallo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill-your-own-work) · ★3)
- **grill-yourself**: Inverted grill — the model interrogates its own plan on the user's behalf, ghost-writing the hard questions in the user's voice and answering each with a recommendation, so the human's job shrinks to rendering verdicts. Use when the user wants a plan stress-tested but doesn't know what questions to ask, says "grill yourself," or asks the model to red-team its own proposal. Checkpoints every verdict to brainstorms/ so the session survives interruption. ([Diviner-Dojo/agent_framework_template](https://github.com/Diviner-Dojo/agent_framework_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill-yourself) · ★4)
- **grill-yourself**: Grill yourself down a plan's whole design tree, deciding what you can and escalating only what needs the user. ([ian-pascoe/dotfiles](https://github.com/ian-pascoe/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_agents/skills/grill-yourself) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **grill-yourself**: Adversarial self-review: the main model submits its own plan/design to a grill-master subagent that interrogates it relentlessly over several rounds — a tough two-way discussion until genuine consensus is reached (or disputes are escalated to the human). Based on mattpocock's grill-me, inverted: the model, not the user, is on the grill. Use when hardening a plan before implementation ("grill yourself", "stress-test this plan"), and as the P1 adversarial-review step of /ship-ticket. ([aiarcheology-com/aiarcheology](https://github.com/aiarcheology-com/aiarcheology/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill-yourself))
- **grill-yourself**: Self-interrogation that resolves the technical forks in a plan YOU must decide, using the user's documented engineering principles/mantras as the adjudicator — the complement to `mattpocock-skills:grill-with-docs` (where the user grills you). Use when the user delegates a technical decision ("you decide", "decide singur", "the technical part is yours", "grill yourself"), or when a design / grill-with-docs session hits a technical branch the user can't adjudicate. ([angeloneacsu/skills](https://github.com/angeloneacsu/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-yourself))
- **grill-yourself-with-docs**: Grill yourself down a plan's whole design tree, writing the glossary and ADRs as decisions settle. ([ian-pascoe/dotfiles](https://github.com/ian-pascoe/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_agents/skills/grill-yourself-with-docs) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **grill-zoom**: Problem-first design interview that works one zoom level at a time. First finds the real problem behind what the user says they want (main driver, desired state, what's in, what's out, and the consequences of each), then walks the solution as a tree, overview first and then one component at a time, always showing where you are and what's left. Use when the user says "grill me", "interview me", "zoom grill", wants to think through a design, architecture, feature or plan before building it, or wants to resume a design tree from an earlier session. ([nebtrx/kamar-taj](https://github.com/nebtrx/kamar-taj/tree/HEAD/skills/grill-zoom))
- **grill-zvit-and-shodennik**: Rigorously audit a generated pre-diploma practice report (звіт з практики) and practice diary (щоденник практики) against the Odesa Polytechnic rules in docs/diploma-rules/references/ZVIT-RULES.md and SHODENNYK-RULES.md (text formatting per docs/diploma-rules/RULES.md), and cross-check their content for consistency with the thesis. Use when the user asks to check, verify, validate, "grill", or review a practice report / diary (звіт / щоденник практики) for compliance. ([DaniilMyron/diplomna_robota](https://github.com/DaniilMyron/diplomna_robota/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/grill-zvit-and-shodennik))
- **grill_me**: Relentlessly interrogate a design to eliminate ambiguity before any planning, requirements, or implementation work begins. Use for "grill me", "grill_me", "disambiguate", "deep-dive", or "interview me about this". Runs a structured, branch-by-branch interview: maps the design tree of major decision branches, walks each branch surfacing options, assumptions, and sub-decisions one at a time, resolves cross-branch dependencies, and confirms explicit shared understanding. Acts as a senior architect conducting a design review — never accepts vague answers, names every decision/assumption/constrain… ([blendsdk/claude-codeops](https://github.com/blendsdk/claude-codeops/tree/HEAD/skills/grill_me) · ★5)
- **grill_me**: Interroga al equipo sin tregua sobre una propuesta de proceso o mejora antes de escribirla a specs. Usar cuando el equipo plantee un cambio de negocio y haya que estresarlo, o diga "interrógame" / "estrésame esta propuesta" / "gríllame". ([Akxlarre/Autoescuela](https://github.com/Akxlarre/Autoescuela/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill_me) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **grill_me**: Interview the user relentlessly about a plan or design until reaching shared understanding, resolving each branch of the decision tree. Use when user wants to stress-test a plan, get grilled on their design, or mentions "grill me". ([wesuuu/runbook](https://github.com/wesuuu/runbook/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/grill_me) · ★1)
- **grill_me**: Interroga al usuario sin tregua para cerrar decisiones — sea una propuesta nueva antes de volverla spec, o las decisiones abiertas de specs ya escritas. Usar cuando el usuario diga "interrógame", "gríllame", "estresá esta propuesta", "resolvamos las dudas", "cerremos las decisiones abiertas", o invoque /grill_me. - **grill_me**: Pressure-test your thinking before you act. Two modes. DECIDE runs the Define-Before-You-Build gate (goal -&gt; current situation -&gt; gap + type -&gt; the bridge / solvable-vs-dream -&gt; reverse-engineer the span -&gt; constraints -&gt; solution paths -&gt; build-vs-buy -&gt; grill the goal AND the solution -&gt; success signal) so you never build the wrong thing. CAPTURE relentlessly interviews you about a process, offer, decision, or area and writes everything to a doc, so nothing tacit gets lost and your next build starts near 90% instead of 70%. Use before building anything, or any time you need to get what is i… ([funnel-futurist/01-ff-aios-starter](https://github.com/funnel-futurist/01-ff-aios-starter/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grill_me) · 4 copies)

- **grillboard**: Grill the user about a plan, decision, or idea while a live board in their browser draws the design tree, the references and the spec as it settles. Use when the user asks for GrillBoard or a visual grill, or wants to see the decisions while being grilled. ([Srcanesen/grillboard](https://github.com/Srcanesen/grillboard/tree/HEAD/skills/grillboard) · ★2)
- **grillcraft**: Compile a completed Grill Me session, transcript, or decision discussion into a durable work item under `.agent/work/`: write `decision.md`, initialize `meta.json`, create an ExecPlan, run configurable `execplan-improve` passes, and invoke Goalcraft to activate a Codex `/goal` that executes the ExecPlan lifecycle. Use after grilling, when the user says "GrillCraft", "turn this grill into a goal", "create the ExecPlan and goal", "run Goalcraft after this grill", or asks for N ExecPlan improvements. ([grp06/useful-codex-skills](https://github.com/grp06/useful-codex-skills/tree/HEAD/grillcraft) · ★142)
- **grilldebug**: Relentlessly interrogate a bug until its root cause is cornered — grillme-style, but for debugging. Explore the code, drive a one-question-at-a-time interview in plan mode down each branch of the failure tree, then run a complete systematic diagnosis yourself. Use when the user wants to be grilled toward a root cause, mentions "grilldebug", or has a stubborn bug that needs methodical RCA rather than a guess. ([wesbragagt/nixos](https://github.com/wesbragagt/nixos/tree/HEAD/home/skills/grilldebug))

- **grilling**: 需求还没定清楚就要动手时用——把待定的决策画成设计树，按「前沿」分轮追问：每轮从前沿取一题、用一张问题卡问（答完再下一张），每题附上你的推荐答案，用户答完重算前沿，前沿清空才算问透。事实是你的活（该派子代理查就派），只有决策才交给用户。触发短语：grill me、先问清楚、需求还没想好、把需求捋清楚、帮我把方案问明白、grilling。在你发现请求缺了「给谁用 / 为什么现在做 / 什么算成功 / 硬约束是什么」中任意一项、而自己正准备用假设填上时，也主动提议走这个流程；DEA 开流程前不清楚时亦须先走本 skill。跳过：需求本来就清楚只是活多、单行或机械改动、纯信息查询、用户已明确说先干起来别问。 ([unknown-jim/ai-skills](https://github.com/unknown-jim/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/ai-skills/grilling) · ★1)

- **grilling**: 在构建或实施前，对已校验的 OpenSpec 资产或用户提供的外部计划执行逐项压力检验：先核实证据，再沿单一依赖分支挑战假设、风险、缺失决策与停止条件，最终给出批准、有条件批准或退回结论；只评审，不代替 OpenSpec 修改资产或替 TDD 实现。 触发条件（满足任一）： 1. 用户要求压力检验已有计划或设计（如“拷问这个计划”“帮我挑战这个设计”） 2. 用户希望在构建前逐项追问关键决策（如“先别实现，看看方案哪里站不住脚”“逐条问到方案能落地”） 3. OpenSpec 的 proposal、specs、design、tasks 草案已 validate 通过，需要在最终批准和 TDD 前完成压力检验 不触发（留给 brainstorming、openspec、spec-driven-workflow、tdd 或 reviewing-code-changes）： - 用户尚无候选方案，只想探索需求或生成新点子，留给 brainstorming - 用户要求创建、修订或校验正式规格资产，留给 openspec - 用户要求实现功能、修复缺陷或直接修改代码，留给 tdd 或对应执行 Skill - 用户要求评审已经实现的代码变更、PR 或测试覆盖，留给 reviewing-code-changes 默认路由：所有 validate 通过的 OpenSpec 完整 change… ([Vitus213/specpatrol](https://github.com/Vitus213/specpatrol/tree/HEAD/src/specpatrol/skillpack/skills/grilling))

- **grilling-dominio**: Use antes de construir ou decompor qualquer funcionalidade, para afiar o domínio. Interroga requisitos, protótipo, código e dados de referência atrás de contradições, ambiguidades e comportamento não documentado, e cristaliza glossário (CONTEXT.md), decisões (docs/adr/), modelo de estados (docs/dominio/) e lista de perguntas em aberto. Acione quando o usuário pedir para "fazer o grilling", "entender/afiar o domínio", "levantar dúvidas de regra de negócio", antes de `decompor-epico`, ou quando surgir regra nova (ex.: treinos, mensalidades, login). ([ThaysaRafaele/Ultimate](https://github.com/ThaysaRafaele/Ultimate/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/grilling-dominio) · ★1)
- **grilling-embedded**: Interview the user relentlessly about a plan, decision, or idea. Use when the user wants to stress-test their thinking, wants an architecture interview before coding, wants to interrogate a firmware design, or uses any 'grill' trigger phrases. ([iharhl/embedded-agent-skills](https://github.com/iharhl/embedded-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-embedded) · ★3)
- **grilling-es**: Entrevista a producto sobre un requerimiento, en rondas de preguntas con respuesta recomendada, hasta que no quede ninguna decisión sin tomar, y cierra con un spec en español sobre una plantilla fija (contexto, historias COMO, QUIERO, PARA con escenarios con nombre en gherkin, estados por vista, reglas, decisiones, fuera de alcance y la tabla de escenarios en pantalla con su slug). Usa Lavish para las rondas cuando la sesión tiene terminal, y el chat cuando no. Úsala siempre que alguien de producto traiga una idea, un pedido, una funcionalidad nueva o un cambio a algo existente y haya que pen… ([grez-lucas/design-flow-skills](https://github.com/grez-lucas/design-flow-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-es))

([yolgu/skill-packs](https://github.com/yolgu/skill-packs/tree/HEAD/common/archive/grilling-final-consolidation) · ★1)
- **grilling-fix**: Triggers alongside the grilling skill whenever the user invokes grilling-fix (e.g. `/grilling /grilling-fix`) or asks to be grilled with lettered options. Overrides only grilling's answer-option format; every question must offer explicit options labeled with letters (A, B, C…), never numbers, plus grilling's usual recommendation, and the user may always answer in their own words. Not for use without grilling, and not a replacement for grilling itself. ([eliasstravik/skills](https://github.com/eliasstravik/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-fix) · ★22)
- **grilling-focused**: Use grilling to ask only the key questions that could change a plan's direction, then summarize for user confirmation. Use when the user wants a focused interview about consequential decisions. ([situ2001/situ2001-plugins](https://github.com/situ2001/situ2001-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-focused) · ★1)
- **grilling-focused-with-docs**: A relentless interview to sharpen a plan or design, which also creates docs (ADR's and glossary) as we go. ([situ2001/situ2001-plugins](https://github.com/situ2001/situ2001-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-focused-with-docs) · ★1)
- **grilling-for-routine**: Use when ask-dora routes a routine idea, when running dora harness new, or when grilling a loop-able idea into a routine. After one good one-pass, may freeze a deterministic step as a Fixed step. ([saif-shines/doraval](https://github.com/saif-shines/doraval/tree/HEAD/skills/grilling-for-routine) · ★11)
